- review侧边木板P2新增首红 **1381/3**：合法完整脚底(2435,640)右鞋被near矩形前景误盖；272合法甲板中心×17脚底采样，pavilion89/near38重叠。owner改原图实体多边形后 **1390/1** 剩near9；独立review原source确认这9对应真实北栏柱根/石座与南桩帽/柱边，先前“一切合法脚底都必须无遮挡”过泛化，不得为了凑绿抹实体。最终仍扫描272×17，原图独立核定三块木板Rect全像素要求alpha0，(666,458)/(859,600)源木板及known side完整17点严格0；仅允许32个确切、独立核定的实体source hit（不泛化整窗），其它新增重叠仍失败。非空/屋顶/柱/右桩/两石座alpha正例保留。
- review进一步找出共享Y710中柱反序，owner按5个真实source底沿631/757/924/713/910分runtime物件与纹理，合并mask仅审计。QA独立逐像素验证5片重复像素0、aggregate alpha重建差0；每柱真实底Y及跨band前后排序通过。实际当前主角条带按hframes/vframes/frame_coords先裁当前帧再映射，朝右站位(2435,677)/(2435,686)/(2435,689)实际opaque alpha交集57/37/7，z84/85/86 vs中柱85；686同band依同parent且Player较后sibling绘于前，689严格高band。首 **1437/2** 是通用base-3在两个左柱落同8px band，揭示共享粒度限制；producer批准非相交通用探针改±9跨band，精确677/686/689不变，未挪base/改nav或共享Field。**8px同band内Player后spawn默认画前，不保证真实底脚前后每1px都正确，不能称逐像素全层级根治。**
- 十张物理1920×1080实机图：`game/build/world_native_pair_landmark_v9_{city,bridge,bridge_front,bridge_back,dock,dock_side,dock_middle_front,ferry,city_arrival,ferry_arrival}.png`。原八图完整保留，新增side(2435,640)及朝右middle_front(2435,686)；dock为水上终点独立照片，ferry保留(2390,735)陆侧入口，不用甲板照片替换入口证据。city/dock/ferry/dock_side/middle_front目检湾水实际包到栈桥、船位于水上、出生仍干岸，side鞋不被木板误盖，中柱前角色正常绘于前；城市铺地和西岩肩虽变化，地表/接地整体仍粗糙，**不作正式视觉批准，正式v12/v13 staging不切换**。
- checker/运行使用现有Godot4.6.2，headless/真实OpenGL3都带Dummy音频；headless保留既有ObjectDB退出告警，GPU仍有GL Texture RID/RenderingServer为空/ObjectDB清理告警，不称无告警、不做音乐听感验收。

| 西北→北中 (508,283)→(516,283) | 山麓岩石/河谷；可通需实桥/路。 |
| 北中→东北 (1020,283)→(1028,283) | 林山下缘；检查崖壁覆盖。 |
| 西北→中西 (217,337)→(217,345) | 西侧河道附近；须查可见路。 |
| 中西→中原 (508,500)→(516,500) | 大体干地候选，需正常镜头连路。 |
| 中原→东岸 (1020,500)→(1028,500) | 大体干地候选，须查树石。 |
| 东岸→东南 (1324,679)→(1324,687) | 海岸岩坡/河边，**高风险**；不能凭旧导航放行。 |
| 中原→江南 (767,679)→(767,687) | 北南桥候选邻新岛/河道；旧桥中心约 (767,683)，需重新量桥长/两岸。 |
| 西南→江南 (508,833)→(516,833) | 旧东西桥跨点落竹林湿地，建议桥中心约 (512,755)；旧 seam 坐标须随桥迁移。 |

候选视觉放行清单：16 个入口各有实体门/码头与干地完整脚底圈，路面从主路连续接门；8 处实际跨缝有路/桥，两侧与导航同源；4 处雪岭/雾岭/桥侧水禁跨；两桥仅板宽可走、板侧 ±16/32px 水不可走、两岸净空且与出生点同域；12 条内部 seam 的正常镜头截图无直线色块/断河；旧档 16 返回锚点和自由坐标迁移后不落水/屋顶。任一未满足都不得替换 v12。

## 2026-09-27 · 榜单验收要同时测UI几何和场景实体入口

- UI合同不仅检查节点存在，还要断言标题/声望/说明不重叠、圆章右界小于文字左界、文字右界小于按钮左界、关闭按钮留在底部牌匾内。
- 城市场景必须逐城验证唯一`bounty_board:<city>`交互、可见实体描述、可点击标记和可行走站位；NPC对白中不得再出现直接开榜动作。
- 本批专项64项（含四城真实Field榜亭节点）、城市运行24项、城市导航109项、通缉循环194项，共391项0失败；严格导入exit0。headless退出仍有既有ObjectDB/资源清理告警。

## 2026-09-27 · 出生点和烘焙物件要测完整人物占地

- “中心像素可走”不能证明出生安全；验收必须用角色脚底椭圆检查中心及周边，特别覆盖桌、香炉、屋檐、围栏和门柱。旧存档错误落点也要经过同一修复函数。
- 门户回归要比较精确源门户锚点，并覆盖真实保存/恢复；当前118条双向路线通过。
- 世界地图v12验收为16入口同域、20路、2开放区、4592中心线脚底样本、36.58%可走率及30块高清分块；门派实际导航126项、本批根治39项均0失败。
- 音频自动化只能确认路由不再复用被否决曲目，不能替代人工试听结论。

## 2026-09-11 · Native Input仿真补验发现双层缺陷（runtime修复后复测通过）

- 最终默认正式场景（不传透明诊断参数）NativePointerEmulation **33/0**：8出口样本各1次激活、8NPC样本各1次请求、4远距样本各1个toast、4道具样本各1次对白且保持打开；普通/quick mouse/touch均通过。原始与模拟事件依然都进入Input，Field正确只执行一次，GUI按钮的触摸仿鼠未受影响。
- 本次全套重跑 **438 checks / 0 failed**：native33、school/pointer182、equipment49、tooltip7、road Python8、world49、paired portal110。strict import exit0，无SCRIPT ERROR。退出资源诊断仍有：native与school各ObjectDB/1 resource、world ObjectDB/3 resources、tooltip与paired ObjectDB；equipment无warning。strict仅已有build/wechat_stage嵌套项目warning。下方保留修前证据，不表示当前残留。

- 新增 `game/tests/test_pointer_native_emulation_20260911.gd`，继承已有真实scene fixture但使用Input.parse_input_event；NativeWitness记录原生/模拟pressed事件，GUI日志记录接收控件。前一轮405项仍只是其已注明的controller输入分支范围，不足以证明原生事件链通过。
- 正式GUI原样跑33项/24失败：所有样本均由`FieldWalkable/filter=0`根Control收到两次gui_input并吞掉，出口/NPC/道具/toast计数为0。不是Headless事件未生成；_input见证器确实收到两个事件。
- 诊断参数`-- --transparent-field-roots`仅在测试内存把根Control和纯Background设IGNORE，不改文件；33项/22失败。出口8样本均2次激活，NPC8样本均2次请求，远距4样本均2个toast。旧账道具4样本均dialog_started=1，但鼠标2样本原生第二事件继续推进并关掉刚开的对白（触摸2样本保持打开），故额外2失败。
- 精确事件序列：物理mouse pressed→InputEventScreenTouch/device=-1→InputEventMouseButton/device=0；物理touch pressed→InputEventMouseButton/device=-1→InputEventScreenTouch/device=0。无等待的quick按下/松开对也同样双发。
- 最小runtime建议交owner：Field根及纯背景MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE；Field未处理输入分支只激活原生指针device>=0，并将device=-1模拟指针标记handled后返回，避免其落入prop Area2D physics input_event。不要全局关闭项目触摸仿鼠，以免破坏Button/装备UI。修后默认模式应33/0，每次出口/NPC/toast为1，道具对白仅开1次且不被同一次点击推进关闭。
- 本补验未改runtime/data，未访问用户存档。仅测试期断言失败，未出现SCRIPT ERROR；退出仍ObjectDB/1 resource警告。

> Role: `qa` / 测试与验收 agent.
> Scope: automated tests, manual checklists, repro records, verification commands, acceptance evidence.
> Canonical docs: `docs/acceptance-checklists/qa.md`, `docs/mvp-m*-checklist.md`.

## QA Responsibilities

- Verify work against acceptance, not against intention.
- Reproduce bugs with steps, expected result, actual result, and logs.
- Do not modify implementation code while acting as QA.
- If acceptance is untestable, return to `producer` for clarification.

## Current Automated Assets

- `game/tests/test_inventory_m5.gd`: inventory use, equipment slots, serialization roundtrip.
- `scripts/smoke_test.py`: low-cost pipeline smoke test.
- `scripts/verify.py`: project verification when applicable.
- `scripts/qa/check_sprite_strip.py`: sprite strip segmentation / baseline / height QA.

## Common Commands

```powershell
# Sprite strip QA
python scripts/qa/check_sprite_strip.py `
  --source assets/raw/sprite/v3/sprite_lengguyun_walk_right_8f_loop_strip.png `
  --expected 9 --baseline-tolerance 12 --height-tolerance 24 `
  --report logs/qa/sprite_walk_right_8f_loop_strip.json
```

Godot test command depends on local `GODOT_BIN`; see `docs/experience-log.md` §14.1.

## Manual Regression Focus

- Main menu: new game / continue game.
- Field: fallback background appears if PNG missing.
- Dialog: choices, side effects, and on_end actions resolve.
- Quest: accept / complete / save / reload persistence.
- Inventory and equipment: slots, use item, save v3 roundtrip.
- Shop: `shop:` actions and `SceneRouter.go_shop()` path.

## Reporting Format

```text
[qa-result]
target: <feature or file>
commands:
  - <command>
result: PASS | FAIL | BLOCKED
evidence:
  - <logs / screenshots / report paths>
notes:
  - <important observation>
```

## Lessons

- A GIF can look wrong because preview frames are cropped inconsistently, not because the source animation is wrong. Verify with fixed-anchor previews.
- `.import` files do not prove PNGs exist.
- QA should preserve failing evidence instead of immediately fixing the implementation.

_Last updated: 2026-04-30_

## 2026-08-14 Sparring / readable-item regression gate

- Test the reward matrix by stable spar ID: loss-loss-win-win, win-loss and two IDs sharing an enemy definition. After first victory every resource delta must remain zero.
- Sparring settlement must not emit ordinary defeat/loot side effects, and must restore the pre-battle HP/MP snapshot. Save JSON round-trip must retain claimed loss/first-win states; missing v8 fields default to fresh.
- Readable KEY_ITEMs require non-empty long text, a scene acquisition path and a modal that opens/closes without consuming the item. Battle reading is disabled.
- Current focused validator: `res://test/validate_city_sparring_and_readables.tscn` (100 checks, 0 failed on 2026-08-14).

_Last updated: 2026-08-14_

## 2026-08-20 · Story catalogue regression gate

- Focused command: `python scripts/validate_story_catalog.py`; it rebuilds the model and compares it with the HTML embedded payload so stale generated pages fail.
- Chapters 1-4 require resolved maps, characters and linked quest stages. Chapters 5-8 require planned stages and zero current runtime stages; their legacy quest count is informational only.
- Core branch counts are chapter 2/3/4 = 2/5/2, matching `ch2_rescue_victory/n4`, `ch3_faction_choice/start`, and `ch4_canal_result/b`.
- Current baseline: chapter flow, chapter 2 and chapter 4 validators pass; chapter 3 faction validator fails because the assembly does not grant the shared official-paper clue. Do not attribute that existing content failure to the story HTML generator.
